````tsx
              !window.confirm('This local text is not the submitted answer. Copy it before continuing if you need it.')
            )
              return;
            onDone();
          }}
        >
          Continue without this copy
        </button>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (blocked) return <UnavailableEntry session={session} blocked={blocked} />;
  if (!attempt || !snapshot || !settings || leaving) return <p>Loading…</p>;

  const total = session.entries.length;
  const previous = index > 0 ? session.entries[index - 1] : undefined;
  const sameStimulus = previous?.taskId.split('.')[0] === entry.taskId.split('.')[0];
  const timed = settings.finalWeeks && settings.timerEnabled;
  const over = elapsed > settings.timerSeconds;
  const tooLong = answer.length > MAX_ANSWER_LENGTH;

  return (
    <>
      <p className="meta">
        Task {index + 1} of {total}
        {attempt.kind === 'review' ? ' · review' : attempt.kind === 'coached' ? ' · try again' : ''}
        {attempt.stimulusSeenBefore ? ' · familiar argument' : ''}
      </p>
      {sameStimulus && <p className="meta">Same argument as the previous task.</p>}
      <Stimulus text={snapshot.stimulus} />
      {snapshot.credit && <p className="meta">Source: {snapshot.credit}</p>}
      <h1 className="task-prompt">{snapshot.prompt}</h1>
      {timed && (
        <p className={over ? 'timer over' : 'timer'} aria-live="off">
          {fmt(elapsed)} / {fmt(settings.timerSeconds)}
          {over ? ' · over time (keep going; it is recorded separately)' : ''}
        </p>
      )}
      <label htmlFor="answer" className="sr-only">
        Your answer
      </label>
      <textarea
        id="answer"
        rows={6}
        value={answer}
        readOnly={conflict}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="Write your answer in your own words."
        aria-describedby="answer-status"
      />
      <p id="answer-status" className="meta" aria-live="polite">
        {saved === 'saving' ? 'Saving…' : saved === 'saved' ? 'Saved' : saved === 'error' ? 'Not saved' : ''}
        {answer.length > MAX_ANSWER_LENGTH * 0.8 && ` · ${answer.length} / ${MAX_ANSWER_LENGTH} characters`}
      </p>
      {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {navigationWarning}
      {conflict &&
        matchesDraftIdentity(liveSession, live, precondition(attempt)) &&
        live?.state === 'draft' &&
        live.revision >= revision.current && (
          <button onClick={() => void loadSavedDraft()}>Use the saved answer</button>
        )}
      <div className="row">
        <button
          className="primary"
          disabled={conflict || tooLong || answer.trim() === ''}
          onClick={() => void submit(answer)}
        >
          Submit
        </button>
        <button
          disabled={conflict}
          onClick={() => {
            if (window.confirm('Submit a blank answer? It will be graded as 0 and scheduled for review.'))
              void submit('');
          }}
        >
          Submit blank
        </button>
        <button disabled={conflict} onClick={() => void skip()}>
          Skip
        </button>
        <button className="link" disabled={conflict} onClick={() => void stop()}>
          Stop for now
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="meta">
        Submitted answers can't be edited. Nothing is graded or revealed until you finish this argument.
      </p>
    </>
  );
}

function UnavailableEntry({
  session,
  blocked,
}: {
  session: SessionRecord;
  blocked: { message: string; otherSessionId?: string };
}) {
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  return (
    <>
      <h1>Cannot open this task</h1>
      <p role="alert">{blocked.message}</p>
      {blocked.otherSessionId && (
        <p>
          <Link to={`/session/${blocked.otherSessionId}`}>Continue the unfinished answer</Link>
        </p>
      )}
      <button
        className="primary"
        disabled={busy}
        onClick={() => {
          setBusy(true);
          void endSession(db, ctx(), session.id).catch((e: unknown) => {
            setBusy(false);
            setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
          });
        }}
      >
        End session and grade
      </button>
      {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
      <p>
        <Link to="/">Home</Link>
      </p>
    </>
  );
}

function GradeBreak({ attempts, onContinue }: { attempts: AttemptRecord[]; onContinue: () => void }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const grade = (mode: 'copy' | 'self') =>
    startGrading(
      attempts.map((a) => a.id),
      mode,
      (to) => void navigate(to),
    ).catch((e: unknown) => setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
  return (
    <>
      <h1>Grade this argument?</h1>
      <p>
        You answered {attempts.length} {attempts.length === 1 ? 'task' : 'tasks'} on this argument. Grade now, or keep
        going and grade at the end.
      </p>
      <div className="row">
        <button className="primary" onClick={() => void grade('copy')}>
          Grade with a chatbot
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => void grade('self')}>Grade it myself</button>
        <button onClick={onContinue}>Continue the session</button>
      </div>
      {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
    </>
  );
}

function fmt(s: number): string {
  return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}:${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

function SessionDone({ session, attempts }: { session: SessionRecord; attempts: (AttemptRecord | undefined)[] }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const submitted = attempts.filter((a): a is AttemptRecord => !!a && a.state === 'submitted');
  const skipped = attempts.filter((a) => a?.state === 'skipped').length;
  const unowned = submitted.filter((a) => a.requestId === null);
  const requestIds = [...new Set(submitted.map((a) => a.requestId).filter((x): x is string => x !== null))];

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session.endedAt) void endSession(db, ctx(), session.id);
  }, [session]);

  const grade = async (mode: 'copy' | 'self') => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await startGrading(
        unowned.map((a) => a.id),
        mode,
        (to) => void navigate(to),
      );
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      <h1>Session done</h1>
      <p>
        {submitted.length} {submitted.length === 1 ? 'answer' : 'answers'} submitted
        {skipped > 0 ? `, ${skipped} skipped` : ''}.
      </p>
      {unowned.length > 0 ? (
        <>
          <p>Grade them now with a chatbot, or against the rubric yourself.</p>
          <div className="row">
            <button className="primary" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void grade('copy')}>
              Grade with a chatbot
            </button>
            <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => void grade('self')}>
              Grade it myself
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      ) : null}
      {requestIds.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <h2>Grading requests</h2>
          <ul className="list">
            {requestIds.map((r) => (
              <li key={r}>
                <Link to={`/request/${r}`}>Open request</Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      )}
      {error && <p role="alert">{error}</p>}
      <p>
        <Link to="/">Home</Link>
      </p>
    </>
  );
}
````

#### `src/ui/pages/SettingsPage.tsx`

````tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import Dexie from 'dexie';
import { content } from '../../content.ts';

import {

  type ImportSummary,
} from '../../storage/backup.ts';
import { DEVICE_SETTINGS, type DataSet, type ReviewLogRecord, type VersionedCard } from '../../domain/records.ts';
import { SCHEDULER_CONFIGS } from '../../domain/schedulerConfig.ts';
import { setTaskControls } from '../../storage/ops.ts';
import { TABLES } from '../../storage/db.ts';
import { ctx, db, findTask, newOpId, saveSetting, useLive, useSettings } from '../runtime.ts';

export function SettingsPage() {

        </label>
        {settings.finalWeeks && (
          <>
            <label className="check">

              />{' '}

            </label>
            <label>
              Target seconds per task{' '}
              <select

              >

              >

                  </option>
                ))}

          </>

        <label>

          >

        </label>
        <label>

          <select value={settings.batchSize} onChange={(e) => void saveSetting('batchSize', Number(e.target.value))}>

        <label>
          Mistake{' '}

            onChange={(e) => void saveSetting('focus', { ...settings.focus, tag: e.target.value || null })}

function Suspended() {
  const suspended = useLive(() => db.taskStates.filter((t) => t.suspended).toArray(), []);
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="suspended">
      <h2 id="suspended">Hidden tasks</h2>
      {!suspended || suspended.length === 0 ? (

      ) : (

          {suspended.map((t) => {

                {found ? found.task.prompt : t.taskId}{' '}
                <button
                  className="link"
                  onClick={() => void setTaskControls(db, ctx(), newOpId(), t.taskId, { suspended: false })}
                >
                  Show again
                </button>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

function download(name: string, text: string) {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([text], { type: 'application/json' }));
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  a.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
}

async function exportNow() {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const file = await exportData(db, now, __COMMIT__.slice(0, 7));
  download(`premise-backup-${now.slice(0, 10)}.json`, JSON.stringify(file));
  // Recorded only once the file has been produced.
  await saveSetting('lastExportAt', now);
}

const LEGACY_PREVIEW_DB = 'premise-preview';

const CARD_KEYS = [
  'schedulerVersion',
  'due',
  'stability',
  'difficulty',
  'elapsed_days',
  'scheduled_days',
  'learning_steps',
  'reps',
  'lapses',
  'state',
  'last_review',
] as const satisfies readonly (keyof VersionedCard)[];

function sameCard(a: VersionedCard | null, b: VersionedCard | null): boolean {
  return a === null || b === null ? a === b : CARD_KEYS.every((key) => a[key] === b[key]);
}

function cardKey(card: VersionedCard | null): string {
  return card === null ? 'null' : JSON.stringify(CARD_KEYS.map((key) => card[key]));
}

/** Early preview v1 omitted review sequence numbers and the version of cardBefore. */
function normalizeLegacyReviewHistory(data: DataSet): DataSet {
  const missingSeq = data.reviewLogs.filter((log) => log.seq === undefined);
  if (missingSeq.length > 0 && missingSeq.length !== data.reviewLogs.length) {
    throw new Error('Old preview data mixes review logs with and without sequence numbers.');
  }
  const historical = missingSeq.length > 0;
  if (!historical && data.reviewLogs.every((log) => !log.cardBefore || log.cardBefore.schedulerVersion)) return data;

  const attempts = new Map(data.attempts.map((attempt) => [attempt.id, attempt]));
  const logs = data.reviewLogs.map((log) => {
    const attempt = attempts.get(log.attemptId);
    if (historical && !attempt?.submittedAt) throw new Error(`Review ${log.id} has no submitted answer.`);
    return {
      ...log,
      // Old undo always restored cardBefore under this log's scheduler version.
      cardBefore: log.cardBefore
        ? { ...log.cardBefore, schedulerVersion: log.cardBefore.schedulerVersion ?? log.schedulerVersion }
        : null,
      // Before seq existed, reviewedAt held the effective scheduler time. The attempt preserves
      // the student's actual submission time; cardAfter.last_review preserves the effective time.
      reviewedAt: historical ? attempt!.submittedAt! : log.reviewedAt,
    };
  });
  if (!historical) return { ...data, reviewLogs: logs };

  const activeByTask = new Map<string, ReviewLogRecord[]>();
  for (const log of logs) {
    if (log.undone) continue;
    const active = activeByTask.get(log.taskId) ?? [];
    active.push(log);
    activeByTask.set(log.taskId, active);
  }
  const chainByTask = new Map<string, ReviewLogRecord[]>();
  for (const [taskId, active] of activeByTask) {
    const byPrevious = new Map<string, ReviewLogRecord[]>();
    for (const log of active) {
      const key = cardKey(log.cardBefore);
      const candidates = byPrevious.get(key) ?? [];
      candidates.push(log);
      byPrevious.set(key, candidates);
    }
    const chain: ReviewLogRecord[] = [];
    let prior: VersionedCard | null = null;
    while (chain.length < active.length) {
      const key = cardKey(prior);
      const matches = byPrevious.get(key);
      if (matches?.length !== 1 || !sameCard(matches[0]!.cardBefore, prior)) {
        throw new Error(`Cannot determine the old review order for ${taskId}.`);
      }
      const log = matches[0]!;
      chain.push(log);
      byPrevious.delete(key);
      prior = { ...log.cardAfter, schedulerVersion: log.schedulerVersion };
    }
    chainByTask.set(taskId, chain);
  }

  // Preserve the approximate cross-task application order. Within a task the saved card chain,
  // rather than wall-clock timestamps or random IDs, determines which review came first.
  const chronological = [...logs].sort((a, b) => a.appliedAt.localeCompare(b.appliedAt) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
  const cursor = new Map<string, number>();
  const ordered = chronological.map((log) => {
    if (log.undone) return log;
    const index = cursor.get(log.taskId) ?? 0;
    cursor.set(log.taskId, index + 1);
    return chainByTask.get(log.taskId)![index]!;
  });
  return { ...data, reviewLogs: ordered.map((log, index) => ({ ...log, seq: index + 1 })) };
}

/** The removed public preview used its own database on the same origin. Inspect it without creating it. */
async function hasLegacyPreviewData(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (__PREVIEW__ || !(await Dexie.exists(LEGACY_PREVIEW_DB))) return false;
  // No declared version: declaring today's schema would upgrade an earlier v1 preview database.
  const legacy = new Dexie(LEGACY_PREVIEW_DB);
  try {
    await legacy.open();
    // Include settings and other records too: a focus note may be the only data worth recovering.
    return (await Promise.all(legacy.tables.map((table) => table.count()))).some((count) => count > 0);
  } catch {
    // If an old database cannot be inspected, still offer the export path rather than hide it.
    return true;
  } finally {
    legacy.close();
  }
}

async function exportLegacyPreview(): Promise<void> {
  if (!(await Dexie.exists(LEGACY_PREVIEW_DB))) throw new Error('The old preview data is no longer present.');
  const legacy = new Dexie(LEGACY_PREVIEW_DB);
  try {
    await legacy.open();
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const present = new Set(legacy.tables.map((table) => table.name));
    const data = (await legacy.transaction('r', legacy.tables, async () =>
      Object.fromEntries(
        await Promise.all(
          TABLES.map(async (name) => [name, present.has(name) ? await legacy.table(name).toArray() : []] as const),
        ),
      ),
    )) as unknown as DataSet;
    const normalized = normalizeLegacyReviewHistory(data);
    const versions = new Set(normalized.cards.map((card) => card.schedulerVersion));
    for (const log of normalized.reviewLogs) {
      versions.add(log.schedulerVersion);
      if (log.cardBefore) versions.add(log.cardBefore.schedulerVersion);
    }
    const stored = new Map(normalized.schedulerConfigs.map((record) => [record.version, record.config]));
    const schedulerConfigs = Object.create(null) as Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;
    for (const version of [...versions].sort()) {
      const config = Object.hasOwn(SCHEDULER_CONFIGS, version) ? SCHEDULER_CONFIGS[version] : stored.get(version);
      if (!config) throw new Error(`Scheduler version ${version} has no stored configuration.`);
      schedulerConfigs[version] = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(config)) as Record<string, unknown>;
    }
    const { schedulerConfigs: _table, settings, ...rest } = normalized;
    const file = {
      app: 'premise',
      schemaVersion: EXPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
      exportedAt: now,
      appVersion: __COMMIT__.slice(0, 7),
      schedulerConfigs,
      ...rest,
      settings: settings.filter((record) => !(DEVICE_SETTINGS as readonly string[]).includes(record.key)),
    };
    download(`premise-preview-recovery-${now.slice(0, 10)}.json`, JSON.stringify(file));
  } finally {
    legacy.close();
  }
}

function Backup({ lastExportAt }: { lastExportAt: string | null }) {
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ data: DataSet; summary: ImportSummary } | null>(null);
  const [problems, setProblems] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [legacyPreviewAvailable, setLegacyPreviewAvailable] = useState(false);
  // One backup action at a time, so "Export current data first" finishes before Replace can start.
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  // Only the latest file selection may show a preview; an earlier, slower check is ignored.
  const selection = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    void hasLegacyPreviewData()
      .then((available) => {
        if (active) setLegacyPreviewAvailable(available);
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => {
        if (active) setStatus(`Could not check old preview data: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);
      });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const running = useRef(false);
  const run = async (action: () => Promise<void>) => {
    if (running.current) return;
    running.current = true;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await action();
    } finally {
      running.current = false;
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const onExport = () =>
    run(async () => {
      try {
        await exportNow();
      } catch (e) {
        setStatus(`Export failed: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);
      }
    });

  const onLegacyExport = () =>
    run(async () => {
      try {
        await exportLegacyPreview();
        setStatus('Old preview backup downloaded.');
      } catch (e) {
        setStatus(`Old preview export failed: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);
      }
    });

  const onFile = async (file: File | undefined) => {
    const token = ++selection.current;
    setProblems([]);
    setPending(null);
    setStatus('');
    if (!file) return;
    try {
      const check = await checkImportFile(file);
      if (token !== selection.current) return;
      if (check.ok) setPending({ data: check.data, summary: check.summary });
      else setProblems(check.problems);
    } catch {
      if (token === selection.current) setProblems(['The backup file could not be read or validated.']);
    }
  };

  const replace = () =>
    run(async () => {
      if (!pending) return;
      try {
        await replaceAll(db, pending.data);
        setPending(null);
        setStatus('Imported. Your previous data was replaced.');
      } catch (e) {
        setStatus(`Import failed and nothing changed: ${e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)}`);
      }
    });

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="backup">
      <h2 id="backup">Backup</h2>
      <p className="meta">
        Everything stays on this device.{' '}
        {lastExportAt ? `Last export: ${new Date(lastExportAt).toLocaleString()}.` : 'No export yet.'}
      </p>
      <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => void onExport()}>
        Export a backup
      </button>
      {legacyPreviewAvailable && (
        <div className="panel">
          <h3>One-time recovery: old preview data</h3>
          <p>
            An earlier preview kept practice data separately on this device. Download its backup to preserve it. This
            does not add it to your current practice data or change either copy.
          </p>
          <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => void onLegacyExport()}>
            Download old preview backup
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
      <h3>Import</h3>
      <p className="meta">Importing replaces all data on this device with the file's contents.</p>
      <label>
        Backup file{' '}
        <input type="file" accept="application/json,.json" onChange={(e) => void onFile(e.target.files?.[0])} />
      </label>
      {problems.length > 0 && (
        <div role="alert">
          <p>This file can't be imported:</p>
          <ul>
            {problems.map((p, i) => (
              <li key={i}>{p}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
      {pending && (
        <div className="panel">
          <p>
            The file has {pending.summary.counts.attempts} answers, {pending.summary.counts.gradings} grades and{' '}
            {pending.summary.counts.cards} scheduled tasks
            {pending.summary.lastActivity
              ? `, last active ${new Date(pending.summary.lastActivity).toLocaleString()}`
              : ''}
            .
          </p>
          {pending.summary.unknownSchedulers.length > 0 && (
            <p className="meta">
              Some reviews use a scheduler this version doesn't know; their history stays readable but can't be
              corrected or undone.
            </p>
          )}
          <div className="row">
            <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => void onExport()}>
              Export current data first
            </button>
            <button className="danger" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void replace()}>
              Replace everything
            </button>
            <button className="link" onClick={() => setPending(null)}>
              Cancel
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {status && <p role="status">{status}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
````

End of packet. Please answer in the response format of section 3.

## Reviewer response

GPT-6 Pro (thinking power 5 of 5) in the owner's ChatGPT account, 2026-10-05, in the same chat as its first review. The device session sent this packet in Brave, through the Claude extension, as one attachment; Pro worked for about 25 minutes. The reply follows verbatim, from the first rule below to the last rule before `## Triage` (SHA-256 of the text between them, without the blank lines next to the rules: `d946e30c2db270d0ba107264aeb846b45d859e96d627c6a62a53cf12f123e930`, 15,913 bytes). It was taken from the copy button's own text: "premise-pr3-fixes-packet" is ChatGPT's citation chip for the packet, and `\u00A0` in FIX-1 is the six-character escape Pro wrote.

---

## 1. New findings

Reviewed `b5187df` against both packets. All eight extracted source files match the blob hashes in the supplied diff. I ran the **36 supplied reply fixtures**, **30,000 generated differential parser cases**, and isolated probes for lifecycle checks, counters, preview preconditions, session identity, repair clearance and recovery normalization. The differential cases preserved scores, statuses, warnings and choice options; complete results for saved v2 requests were unchanged.

I did **not** rerun the full application, browser or deployment suites. The 318 unit tests and 66 Chromium/WebKit tests are the coding agent’s reported results, not independently reproduced results from this review. premise-pr3-fixes-packet

### FIX-1 — major — The new heading recognizer loses previously supported formatting and merges rows’ feedback

**Where:** `src/domain/scoreParser.ts:367–400, 450–469, 494–498`.

**Problem:** Switching bounded heading detection from the cleaned line to a new raw-line recognizer loses two previously supported transformations: removing paired outer pipes and recognizing a bullet followed by protocol whitespace other than an ordinary space or tab. An unrecognized second heading neither receives feedback nor terminates the first row’s range. This is a regression introduced by the fix, rather than just an uncovered old limitation. premise-pr3-fixes-packet premise-pr3-fixes-packet

**Reproduced scenario:** Inside one valid request-bound feedback section, with the score block giving I01 `2/2` and I02 `1/3`:

```text
I01: 2/2
- First criterion met.
| I02: 1/3 |
- Tip: Second row tip.
```

Parser v3 separates the rows correctly. Parser v4 gives **all four lines to I01** and leaves I02 unmatched. The result can therefore show I02’s tip as I01’s Correction.

The same regression occurs with `-\u00A0I02: 1/3`: a bullet followed by a non-breaking space. The old `cleanLine` accepts that whitespace; `FEEDBACK_LIST_MARKER` does not.

The gap check also regresses. With otherwise normal bounded feedback, putting `| I01: 0/2 |` between `END FEEDBACK` and the score block no longer invalidates attribution. Parser v3 leaves feedback unmatched for that ambiguous reply; v4 attaches it. Both failures follow from using the incomplete recognizer for boundaries and gap detection. premise-pr3-fixes-packet premise-pr3-fixes-packet

**Proposed fix:** Preserve the bounded reader’s previously supported presentation cleanup—including paired outer pipes and protocol whitespace—while retaining raw offsets and separate quotation/fence eligibility. Keep this change out of shared `cleanLine` and score parsing.

Add fixtures for the pipe-wrapped heading both inside the section and in the gap, plus the non-breaking-space bullet. The inside-section tests should assert that I01’s range ends before I02, not merely that both scores still parse.

### FIX-2 — major — A quotation nested inside a list still fails to bound the preceding row

**Where:** `src/domain/scoreParser.ts:367–371, 460–469`.

**Problem:** The recognizer removes quotation prefixes first, then removes one list marker, but never checks for another quotation prefix. Consequently, `- > I02: 1/3` and `> - > I02: 1/3` return no heading at all. The literal `>> I02:` case is fixed, but the combination of supported container markers still lets another row’s text enter the preceding row’s feedback. premise-pr3-fixes-packet

**Reproduced scenario:**

```text
I01: 2/2
- First criterion met.
- > I02: 1/3
  - Tip: Quoted student tip.
```

With valid surrounding feedback markers and the same score block as FIX-1, v4 attributes the entire excerpt to I01. Replacing the quoted heading with `> - > I02: 1/3` has the same result.

The required safe outcome is to end I01’s range at the quoted I02 heading and leave I02 unmatched. Recognizing that boundary must not turn the quotation into attributable feedback. That distinction is already implemented for simpler quoted headings, but these combinations bypass it. premise-pr3-fixes-packet premise-pr3-fixes-packet

**Proposed fix:** Consume the supported leading list and quote markers in a small bounded-reader prefix scanner, remembering whether **any** consumed container was a quotation. Use the remaining row-shaped text as a boundary, but never as attributable feedback when that quotation flag is set.

Add fixtures for a quote within a list and a list within a quote followed by a deeper quote. Assert the preceding row’s exact range and the quoted row’s unmatched status. This does not require a general Markdown parser or another dependency.

### FIX-3 — minor — Emphasis within a heading’s fraction still bypasses the contradiction check

**Where:** `src/domain/scoreParser.ts:356–363, 402–410, 485–493`.

**Problem:** `skipScoreDecoration` removes decoration only before the fraction. Decoration around an individual numerator or denominator prevents `HEADING_SCORE` from recognizing the fraction. The reader then treats the heading as having no score and attaches its assessment without checking for a contradiction. The four specifically requested examples are fixed, but C7’s underlying failure remains for another straightforward formatting combination. premise-pr3-fixes-packet premise-pr3-fixes-packet premise-pr3-fixes-packet

**Reproduced scenario:** Either of these headings, followed by an explanation, is attached to an I01 score-block result of `2/2`:

```text
I01: **0**/2
```

```text
I01: 0/**2**
```

In both probes, I01 keeps the zero-credit assessment rather than being left unmatched. Its numeric grade remains `2/2`; the defect is the contradictory attached feedback.

**Proposed fix:** Recognize supported decoration around the fraction’s individual components, or distinguish **no score present** from **a score-shaped prefix that cannot be safely interpreted**. The latter should leave feedback unmatched rather than silently disable the contradiction check.

Keep normalization limited to the heading’s assessment prefix, not arbitrary explanatory prose. Add contradictory and matching examples, including a decorated denominator.

Ship FIX-1 through FIX-3 together under the next parser version with the corresponding protocol and decision updates. No prompt text, score grammar or storage-schema change is needed.

### FIX-4 — minor — The “submitted in another tab” recovery disappears after that tab grades with Hard or Easy

**Where:** `src/ui/pages/SessionPage.tsx:267–305`; `src/storage/ops.ts`, `matchesDraftIdentity` and `applyGrade`.

**Problem:** Passing `allowEnded = true` fixes the ended-session part of C2, but the read-only completed-attempt view still uses the draft identity predicate. That predicate requires `ratingChoice` to equal the editor’s original value. Grading legitimately changes that field, so a completed attempt can stop matching its original editor without any replacement or identity change. premise-pr3-fixes-packet premise-pr3-fixes-packet

**Concrete scenario:**

Tab A retains a local copy of the last answer, whose original `ratingChoice` is `good`. Tab B submits the answer and ends the session. Tab A can now show the intended “submitted in another tab” view. Before continuing in A, the student confirms full credit in B and selects “That was hard” or “Too easy.”

Once A receives that update, `matchesDraftIdentity(..., true)` returns false because the stored preference is now `hard` or `easy`. The `takenElsewhere` branch disappears; verification instead declares that the session was replaced, leaving the conflict editor without the intended “Continue without this copy” control. I reproduced the predicate transition for both preferences. The branch and error selection follow directly from the supplied component code. premise-pr3-fixes-packet

The local text remains present, and this is not an overwrite finding. It is a false conflict and loss of the normal continuation path after a legitimate cross-tab workflow.

**Proposed fix:** For the read-only submitted/discarded view, compare genuinely fixed identity fields rather than the original draft’s grading preference. Keep the strict draft preconditions—including the original preference where appropriate—for operations that can write draft text.

Extend the one-task and two-task browser cases through **submission, session ending and grading with Hard/Easy**, then verify that A retains its local text and its continuation control. The current tests stop after submission and ending, before the field changes. premise-pr3-fixes-packet

I found no additional defect in the new reply-content comparison for chosen blocks, the source-specific preview requirements, or the manual/self resolution and correction paths inspected. The recovery normalization is additional code, but its purpose is bounded: reconstruct missing historical export fields without modifying the old database. The historical record definition confirms the missing sequence and `cardBefore` version fields it handles. premise-pr3-fixes-packet premise-pr3-fixes-packet 

The disclosure change is in `RequestPage.CopySection`, rather than Settings. It preserves the clipboard call inside the click gesture, waits for persistence before completing the explicit “show” path, and exposes a persistence error instead of silently ignoring it. I found no merge-blocking issue in that change. premise-pr3-fixes-packet

The remaining fixes should stay local to bounded feedback recognition and the completed-attempt display predicate. They do not justify a new dependency, schema migration, synchronization layer or general Markdown engine.

**Verdict: merge after fixes.**

## Triage

Proposed by Claude on 2026-10-05. The owner implements PR #3, so each "accepted" below is a recommendation for that PR until the owner records the change.

Claude checked `b5187df` independently while Pro worked, with three agents: storage, import, confirmation and planning; the session editor, request page and Settings; and the parser.
- Each agent ran the relevant tests and reverted every fix to confirm its test then fails.
- Each probed the findings' scenarios through the app's real operations.
- The parser agent also compared 1.1 million generated replies between the parsers at `b86b0df` and `b5187df`. It found no difference in scores, statuses, choices or legacy v2 feedback.

Its findings are V1 to V14 below.

Every Pro finding was reproduced:
- FIX-1 to FIX-3 with parser probes against `b86b0df` and `b5187df`;
- FIX-4 by reading the code (`ops.ts:125` compares `ratingChoice`, and grading writes it at `ops.ts:722`).

PR #3 merged with today's `main` (`b521aab`, exercise batch 2) is conflict-free and passes `npm run check` (318 unit tests) and the Chromium browser tests (33 of 33).

Both checks find the round-1 fixes sound apart from the gaps below, and both say merge after one more short round. No fix needs a schema change. Pro suggests a new parser version for FIX-1 to FIX-3, but parser 4 has never been deployed, so these changes stay in version 4. The owner's coding agent gets the fixes as a prompt that numbers them as "items".

### Round-1 findings

| # | Status | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | fixed | `backup.ts:171` no longer caps reply text at import. A backup exported by the live release's own code, holding a 250,000-character reply, imports and round-trips. Putting the cap back fails the new test. |
| PR3-1 | fixed | Both guards treat a pending check or a live mismatch as unsaved (`SessionPage.tsx:146-158`, `:185-199`). Removing either guard fails the browser test. |
| C2 | partly fixed | `allowEnded` is passed at `:267-271` and `:292`, and reverting it fails both browser tests. Grading that answer with Hard or Easy in the other tab still turns the view into a false conflict (FIX-4). |
| PR3-2 | fixed | The reply text is compared inside the transaction (`ops.ts:843`). The finding's own case, a replace-import turning "Name the gap" into "Hide the gap", is refused with every table unchanged. |
| PR3-7 | fixed | Required in the type (`ops.ts:567-582`) and at runtime (`:741-751`). Manual and self grades don't need the fields. |
| PR3-3 | partly fixed | The named forms pass. Headings in table pipes or after a non-breaking space regressed (FIX-1), and quotes inside list items still merge rows (FIX-2). |
| C6 | fixed | Fences opened after `-`, `*`, `+`, `•` or a numbered marker close at their indented closer. A non-breaking space after the marker is part of FIX-1. |
| C7 | partly fixed | The four required forms are checked. Emphasis inside the fraction (FIX-3) or underscore emphasis around it (V2) still skips the check. |
| PR3-4 / C3 | fixed | `integrity.ts:155-157`. An ended session with an unopened entry and submitted answers imports, and its answers can be graded. |
| PR3-5 / C4 | fixed | `integrity.ts:177-179`. For a submitted answer, 2,000 characters is accepted and 2,001 refused. Long drafts and skipped answers round-trip. |
| PR3-6 | fixed | Session IDs must be lower-case UUIDs (`integrity.ts:147`), and empty IDs are refused (`:75`). |
| C5 | fixed | Request IDs are checked strictly in lower case (`integrity.ts:35`, `:238`). |
| PR3-8 | fixed | `nextCounter` stops at `MAX_SAFE_INTEGER - 1`, which import accepts. The next increment is refused with nothing written. |
